[EVENT: IRQ48099]
Date: 2023-08-12
Country: Iraq | Location: Gire Cudi
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PKK: Kurdistan Workers Party [Rebel group]
Actor2: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) [External/Other forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ANF News [International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 12 August 2023, PKK and PKK-YJA STAR militants attacked Turkish positions via firearms in Gire Cudi area in Al Amadiya district, Duhok in conjunction with airstrikes carried out by Turkish army warplanes. HPG (PKK) claimed 4 soldiers were killed and one soldier was injured in the attacks by PKK-YJA STAR but this was not confirmed by other sources (PKK's claim of fatalities among Turkish forces is coded as 0 fatalities). A Turkish surveillance camera system was destroyed in the attack by PKK.

---

[EVENT: IRQ48226]
Date: 2023-08-15
Country: Iraq | Location: Basrah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mirbad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 15 August 2023, employees of the Electricity Department protested in front of the Electricity Transmission Company building in Basrah city. The protesters demanded the company acknowledge their certifications the same way they are accepted in other departments. The protesters demanded the certifications be taken into account when assigning salaries and work hours. The protesters stated they have been working on permanent contracts for 4 years and have provided uncompensated service because of the certification issue. The protesters added 15,000 workers are affected by the issue.

[EVENT: IRQ48859]
Date: 2023-09-26
Country: Iraq | Location: Al Islah
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Al Omari Tribal Militia (Iraq) [Identity militia]
Actor2: Al Rumaid Tribal Militia (Iraq) [Identity militia]
Fatalities: 2
Sources: Baghdad Today; Shafaaq News [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 26 September 2023, overnight Rumaid and Omari tribal militants clashed on the outskirts of Al Islah, Thi Qar. Dozens of militants clashed with mortars and small arms. The clash was motivated by a dispute over the Qaymaqam's seat after the Thi Qar governor refused to dismiss the current Qaymaqam affiliated with the Rumaid tribe. The PMF erected checkpoints on the outskirts of Al Islah. Rumaid tribal militants erected roadblocks on the Al Islah - Nassriya road and began checking ID cards in search of Omari tribesmen. In April 2023, the same tribes clashed due to a dispute related to the Qaymaqam's seat. The Iraqi army and police deployed to the city to restore order. The clashes resulted in 2 fatalities and 3 injured.

[EVENT: IRQ48865]
Date: 2023-09-29
Country: Iraq | Location: Amadiya
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: PKK: Kurdistan Workers Party [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 3
Sources: ANF News; People's Defense Forces [Other-International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 29 September 2023, Turkish artillery, tanks, and heavy weapons carried out shelling against PKK forces in Sehid Delil area (Al Amadiya district), Duhok. HPG (PKK) later confirmed that 3 PKK militants were killed in the Zap region that day (presumably referring to this event). 3 fatalities.

---

Country: Iraq | Location: Sigiri
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) [External/Other forces]

[EVENT: IRQ48883]
Date: 2023-09-29
Country: Iraq | Location: Gire Carcel
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PKK: Kurdistan Workers Party [Rebel group]
Actor2: Military Forces of Turkey (2016-) [External/Other forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ANF News [International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 29 September 2023, PKK militants attacked Turkish soldiers via firearms and heavy weapons in Gire Carcel area in Al Amadiya district, Duhok, in conjunction with shelling and airstrikes carried out by Turkish forces. HPG claimed chemical weapons were used by Turkish army during shelling but other sources did not verify this. Casualties unknown.

---

[EVENT: IRQ48890]
Date: 2023-09-29
Country: Iraq | Location: Amara
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mirbad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 29 September 2023, teachers protested in front of the directorate of education in Amara city. The protesters area teachers assigned to rural schools and demanded to be transferred to schools in Amara city. The protesters stated some of them have been assigned to rural schools for 10 years and have to travel over 100km to reach work.

---

[EVENT: IRQ48891]
Date: 2023-09-30
Country: Iraq | Location: Rawah
Type: Strategic developments > Disrupted weapons use | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Military Forces of Iraq (2022-2026) Popular Mobilization Forces [State forces]
Actor2: Islamic State in Iraq and the Levant (ISIL) [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Maloomah [National]

DESCRIPTION:
Weapons seizure: On 30 September 2023, the Popular Mobilization Forces carried out a security operation and discovered a cache of weapons belonging to IS militants in Rawah district, Al Anbar. The weapons included a large quantity of TNT and C4, explosive rulers, and oxygen gas bottles, in addition to military projectiles.
